Eksempel med ny case

Risiko for barn nummer 2

  • Hvad kan forklare, at en person får barn nummer 2?

Vi er altså interesseret i forløbet mellem barn 1 og 2 (tvillinger tæller som ét barn)

  1. Hvad er det første vi skal tænkte på iht. et forløbs start?

  2. Er det andet der kunne komplicere analysen iht. data og definition af event?

Generel fremgang

  1. Definér, hvornår den enkelte person, er under risiko for at opleve hændelsen.

  2. Fasthold alle observationer, hvor personen er i populationen under risiko.

  3. Overvej, hvilke kovariater, der er relevante at inddrage i undersøgelsen.

  4. Overvej om de skal være tidsvarierende eller ikke-tidsvarierende.

  5. Lav Kaplan-Meier (sammenlign grupper).

  6. Lav Cox-model.

  • Inddrag tidsvarierende variable.

  • Lav modelkontrol.

  1. Lav ventetidsmodeller.
  • Bestem, hvilken fordeling, der fitter data bedst.

Indlæs pakker

```r

library(survival)
library(tidyverse)
library(survminer)

options(scipen = 999)

<!-- rnb-source-begin eyJkYXRhIjoiYGBgclxuYGBgclxuXG5kZjEgPC0gZGYgJT4lIFxuICBtdXRhdGUoYWdlID0gYXMubnVtZXJpYyhBTERFUikpICU+JVxuICBmaWx0ZXIoYWdlID49IDE4KSAlPiUgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgIFxuICBtdXRhdGUocGVyc29uX3N0YXJ0ID0gY2FzZV93aGVuKGxhZyhwbnIpID09IHBuciB+IDAsIFRSVUUgfiAxKSkgJT4lIFxuICBtdXRhdGUoYm9ybiA9IGFzLm51bWVyaWMoQ19BTlRCT0VSTkYpKSAlPiUgXG4gIG11dGF0ZShrdmluZGUgPSBpZl9lbHNlKEtPRU4gPT0gMiwgMSwgMCkpICU+JVxuICBzZWxlY3QoLSBDX0FOVEJPRVJORiwgLSBLT0VOLCAtQUxERVIpICBcblxuYGBgXG5gYGAifQ== -->

```r
```r

df1 <- df %>% 
  mutate(age = as.numeric(ALDER)) %>%
  filter(age >= 18) %>%                                               
  mutate(person_start = case_when(lag(pnr) == pnr ~ 0, TRUE ~ 1)) %>% 
  mutate(born = as.numeric(C_ANTBOERNF)) %>% 
  mutate(kvinde = if_else(KOEN == 2, 1, 0)) %>%
  select(- C_ANTBOERNF, - KOEN, -ALDER)  


<!-- rnb-source-end -->

<!-- rnb-chunk-end -->

### Dan event-variablen og start/slut-tider 


<!-- rnb-text-end -->


<!-- rnb-chunk-begin -->


<!-- rnb-source-begin eyJkYXRhIjoiYGBgclxuYGBgclxuXG5kZjIgPC0gZGYxICU+JSBcbiAgbXV0YXRlKGV2ZW50ID0gaWZfZWxzZShsYWcoYm9ybikgPCBib3JuICBcbiAgICAgICAgICAmIHBlcnNvbl9zdGFydCA9PSAwIHwgcGVyc29uX3N0YXJ0ID09IDEgJiBib3JuID49IDEsIDEsIDApKSAgIFxuXG5kZjMgPC0gZGYyICU+JSAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgXG4gIG11dGF0ZShzdGFydCA9IGFnZSAtIDE4KSAlPiUgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICBcbiAgbXV0YXRlKHNsdXQgPSBhZ2UgLSAxNylcblxuYGBgXG5gYGAifQ== -->

```r

df2 <- df1 %>% 
  mutate(event = if_else(lag(born) < born  
          & person_start == 0 | person_start == 1 & born >= 1, 1, 0))   

df3 <- df2 %>%                                                        
  mutate(start = age - 18) %>%                                                
  mutate(slut = age - 17)

### Variablen period_slut dannes skal senere bruges til at fastholde observationen af afslutningen inden for den enkelte enhed (personen) til analyse af multiple hændelsesforløb.


<!-- rnb-text-end -->


<!-- rnb-chunk-begin -->


<!-- rnb-source-begin eyJkYXRhIjoiYGBgclxuYGBgclxuXG5kZjQgPC0gZGYzICU+JVxuICBtdXRhdGUobmV3ID0gY2FzZV93aGVuKHBlcnNvbl9zdGFydCA9PSAxIH4gMCwgXG4gICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgIHBlcnNvbl9zdGFydCAhPSAxIH4gbGFnKGV2ZW50KSkpICU+JSBcbiAgYXJyYW5nZShwbnIsIGFhcikgJT4lXG4gIGdyb3VwX2J5KHBucikgJT4lXG4gIG11dGF0ZShwZXJpb2QgPSBjdW1zdW0obmV3KSsxKSAlPiUgIFxuICBzZWxlY3QoLW5ldyklPiUgXG4gIG11dGF0ZShwZXJzb25fc2x1dCA9IGNhc2Vfd2hlbihsZWFkKHBucikgPT0gcG5yIH4gMCwgVFJVRSB+IDEpKSAlPiVcbiAgbXV0YXRlKHBlcmlvZF9zbHV0ID0gY2FzZV93aGVuKGV2ZW50ID09IDEgfCBwZXJzb25fc2x1dCA9PSAxIH4gMSwgVFJVRSB+IDApKSAlPiVcbiAgbXV0YXRlKGlndWQgPSBhcy5mYWN0b3IoY2FzZV93aGVuKGlfdWRkID09IDEgfCBpX3VkZCA9PSAyIHxcbiAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgIGlfdWRkID09IDMgfCBpX3VkZCA9PSA0IH4gXFx1bmRlcl91ZGRcXCAsXG4gICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICAgICBUUlVFIH4gXFxpa2tlX2lnYW5nXFwpKSkgXG5cbmBgYFxuYGBgIn0= -->

```r
```r

df4 <- df3 %>%
  mutate(new = case_when(person_start == 1 ~ 0, 
                          person_start != 1 ~ lag(event))) %>% 
  arrange(pnr, aar) %>%
  group_by(pnr) %>%
  mutate(period = cumsum(new)+1) %>%  
  select(-new)%>% 
  mutate(person_slut = case_when(lead(pnr) == pnr ~ 0, TRUE ~ 1)) %>%
  mutate(period_slut = case_when(event == 1 | person_slut == 1 ~ 1, TRUE ~ 0)) %>%
  mutate(igud = as.factor(case_when(i_udd == 1 | i_udd == 2 |
                                    i_udd == 3 | i_udd == 4 ~ \under_udd\ ,
                                    TRUE ~ \ikke_igang\))) 


<!-- rnb-source-end -->

<!-- rnb-chunk-end -->

```r

df5 <- df4 %>% 
  filter(person_start == 1) %>% 
  select(pnr, region) %>% 
  mutate(region18 = as.factor(case_when(region == 1 ~ \Hovedstaden\, 
                                        region == 2 ~ \Sjælland\,
                                        region == 3 ~ \Syddanmark\,
                                        region == 4 ~ \Midtjylland\,
                                        region == 5 ~ \Nordjylland\))) %>% 
  select(-region)

### Konstruér startalder for hver periode


<!-- rnb-text-end -->


<!-- rnb-chunk-begin -->


<!-- rnb-source-begin eyJkYXRhIjoiYGBgclxuYGBgclxuXG5kZjcgPC0gZGY2ICU+JSBcbiAgbXV0YXRlKHBlcmlvZGVfc3RhcnQgPSBjYXNlX3doZW4obGFnKGV2ZW50ID09IDEpICYgcGVyc29uX3N0YXJ0ID09IDAgfCAgcGVyc29uX3N0YXJ0ID09IDEgfiAxLCBUUlVFIH4gMCkpICU+JSBcbiAgZmlsdGVyKHBlcmlvZGVfc3RhcnQgPT0gMSkgJT4lIFxuICBtdXRhdGUocGVyaW9kZV9zdGFydGFhciA9IGFhciwgcGVyaW9kZV9hZ2UgPSBhZ2UpICU+JSBcbiAgc2VsZWN0KHBuciwgcGVyaW9kLCBwZXJpb2RlX3N0YXJ0YWFyLCBwZXJpb2RlX2FnZSlcblxuYGBgXG5gYGAifQ== -->

```r
```r

df7 <- df6 %>% 
  mutate(periode_start = case_when(lag(event == 1) & person_start == 0 |  person_start == 1 ~ 1, TRUE ~ 0)) %>% 
  filter(periode_start == 1) %>% 
  mutate(periode_startaar = aar, periode_age = age) %>% 
  select(pnr, period, periode_startaar, periode_age)


<!-- rnb-source-end -->

```r

multi_all <- mutate(df8, 
                    pt_start = aar - periode_startaar,
                    pt_slut = aar - periode_startaar + 1) %>% 
  select(pnr, aar, event, start, slut, person_slut, 
         period_slut, periode_startaar, periode_age, 
         pt_start, pt_slut,
         everything())

                data = multi_all2)
